<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the full picture. Let me find the key experiment for the LLaMA analysis:

**exp_007_llama_inference** (seed 42, LLaMA SciERC):
- Command: `python run_mvp_pilot.py --model_path checkpoints/llama3.1-8b-scierc-merged --data_dir data/scierc/processed_data --dataset scierc --subtask joint --n_samples 8 --temperature 1.0 --seed 42 --num_test 9999 --collect_logprobs --output_dir output/exp007_llama_inference/`


- This experiment doesn't specify max_tokens, so it defaults to 512 from before the D054 fix, whereas the Qwen analysis correctly used exp_012_rerun_1024 with --max_tokens 1024 for the same seed. The unregistered exp_018_llama_scierc experiments for seeds 123 and 456 have unknown max_tokens values.

If those exp_018 runs were part of a post-D054 batch, they likely used the updated default of 1024, which means the LLaMA 3-seed analysis is mixing pre-fix data (seed 42 at 512 tokens) with potentially post-fix data (seeds 123 and 456 at 1024 tokens). Since D054 showed that the 512-token limit caused truncation artifacts that inflated certain metrics like voting_conf, this inconsistency is a real methodological issue. The registered exp_007 experiments for seeds 123 and 456 exist separately, so I need to check what parameters they actually used.

Looking back at the exp_018 runs, I don't see explicit max_tokens fields in the report.json, and the run logs seem to contain output from the earlier exp_007 experiments rather than new runs. The key question is whether the script's default max_tokens changed at some point, or if exp_018 used a different default altogether.

What really matters is whether the analysis script is pulling from the right data. The LLaMA analysis maps seed 42 to `exp007_llama_inference` (no explicit max_tokens, likely defaulting to 512), while the Qwen analysis is more deliberate, mapping seed 42 to `exp_012_rerun_1024` with explicit 1024 tokens. The LLaMA mappings for seeds 123 and 456 point to unregistered exp_018 runs with unknown parameters, which is less reliable. That said, the D054 truncation issue probably had less impact on LLaMA's joint outputs for SciERC since they tend to be shorter than Qwen's.

Looking at the sanity check values in the LLaMA script, the reported SJ ρ of 0.3630 matches the JSON result for seed 42, which should align with whatever the original exp007_llama_inference report documented. I have what I need to write the review comparing both 3-seed summaries.

The key findings are: LLaMA seed 42 uses a different max_tokens default than the Qwen analysis, creating inconsistency both within the LLaMA seeds and across model comparisons; the exp_018 experiments remain unregistered despite both workers completing; and there's a VC issue that's still unresolved.

Now looking at the metric comparisons, voting confidence outperforms SJ on NER ρ for both models (Qwen: 0.386 vs 0.364, LLaMA: 0.402 vs 0.362), which challenges the paper's framing of SJ, EM, and VC as an equivalent family. The paper would need a stronger justification for SJ beyond its conditional RE advantages. However, SJ's ρ values show remarkable cross-model stability—Qwen at 0.364 and LLaMA at 0.362—with LLaMA's variance being even tighter than Qwen's, suggesting the metric is robust across seeds.

For RE, both models confirm SJ is the weaker approach compared to logprob, and there's an unresolved discrepancy in how AUROC is being computed between the analysis script and the pipeline's registered values that needs clarification for the paper.
```

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — worker_analysis_3seed_llama_scierc + Qwen/LLaMA 3-seed 对比审查\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 背景\n\nLLaMA SciERC 3-seed 分析 worker 完成，在 `review_round2/llama_scierc_3seed_summary.json` 生成了 LLaMA 3-seed 汇总（与此前 Qwen 的 `qwen_scierc_3seed_summary.json` 配对）。以下对两份 summary 做联合审查。\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **LLaMA seed 42 max_tokens 不一致**：LLaMA 分析脚本使用 `exp007_llama_inference` 作为 seed 42 数据源，其命令 **未指定 --max_tokens**（默认 512，D054 前的旧行为）。而 Qwen 分析的 seed 42 正确使用了 `exp_012_rerun_1024`（`--max_tokens 1024`）。D054 已确认 max_tokens=512 会造成 truncation artifacts（voting_conf 虚高、logprob 被压低）。这意味着：(a) LLaMA 3-seed 内部 seed 42 与 seeds 123/456 的截断行为可能不同；(b) Qwen vs LLaMA 的跨模型对比存在系统偏差（Qwen=1024 vs LLaMA seed42=512）。**建议：对 LLaMA seed 42 补做 max_tokens=1024 重跑（类似 Qwen 的 exp_012_rerun_1024），或至少确认 exp_018 seeds 123/456 的 max_tokens 参数**。\n\n- [severity: warning] **Registry 缺口未修复**：此前已报告的 exp_018 系列 4 个实验仍未补录 registry。两个分析 worker 均完成但都未创建 registry 条目。分析脚本依赖未注册数据（exp_018_{qwen,llama}_scierc_seed{123,456}）生成 3-seed summary，这些 summary 如用于论文，溯源链断裂。\n\n- [severity: warning] **NER: Voting Confidence 一致性地强于 SJ**。两个模型均显示 VC > SJ：\n  - Qwen: VC ρ=0.386±0.019 > SJ ρ=0.364±0.018\n  - LLaMA: VC ρ=0.402±0.012 > SJ ρ=0.362±0.005\n  \n  Paper framing (Claim 1) 将 SJ、EM、VC 定位为 \"structural consistency signal family\"。但如果审稿人问\"SJ 既然不是最强的，为什么要提出？\"，论文需要明确回答 SJ 的独特价值（如对任意结构输出的泛化性）。当前 framing 把 VC 和 SJ 并列没有问题，但不能暗示 SJ 是最优 signal。\n\n- [severity: info] **跨模型一致性极好**：NER SJ ρ Qwen=0.364 vs LLaMA=0.362（几乎完全相同），且 LLaMA 的 cross-seed std=0.005 极低。支撑 \"model-agnostic\" claim。\n\n- [severity: info] **RE 两模型均弱**：Qwen RE SJ ρ=0.249, LLaMA RE SJ ρ=0.277，均为最弱非平凡 signal（低于 FK、logprob、VC）。与 D008/D009 决策一致。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. **优先级最高**：补做 LLaMA SciERC seed 42 的 max_tokens=1024 重跑（对齐 Qwen 的 exp_012_rerun_1024 方法），或者改用已有的 exp_007_llama_seed123/456（它们也是 512 默认）+ 说明所有 LLaMA 数据均为 512。但混用 512/1024 是最差选择。\n2. 将 exp_018 系列 4 个实验补录 registry，包含 command、max_tokens、seed 等完整 spec。\n3. 论文 Claim 1 叙述中明确 SJ 的 positioning vs VC：SJ 不是 NER 上最强 signal，但其结构定义使其可泛化到任意 structured output 任务（VC 依赖于 entity 表面形式的精确匹配，对 RE 等任务不如 SJ 稳定）。",

   - `worker_provenance_exp_018_registry` — supposed to fix registry gaps I flagged

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Structural consistency signals**: SJ (Soft Jaccard), FK (Fleiss' Kappa), EM (Exact Match), logprob (log-probability), VC (Voting Confidence) — 5 unsupervised quality estimation signals
   - **Spearman ρ correlation** between signal and per-instance F1 as primary metric
   - **AUROC** for binary discrimination quality
   - **D054 max_tokens fix**: max_tokens=512 caused truncation artifacts; corrected to 1024. voting_conf was inflated, logprob suppressed under 512
   - **Joint vs NER-only subtask modes**: joint includes NER+RE; RE consistently fails (structural signal weak)
   - **3-seed protocol**: seeds 42, 123, 456 for robustness validation
   - **Cross-model validation**: Qwen3-8B vs LLaMA-3.1-8B on SciERC
   - **Paper Claim 1**: structural consistency signals as quality estimators (EMNLP 2026, ARR May deadline 2026-05-25)
   - **Registry tracking protocol**: all experiments must be registered with full spec before conclusions can be cited

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **Registry (registry.yaml)**: 73 total entries, NO exp_018 entries exist despite 4 exp_018 experiments having completed on server. This is the central tracking gap.
   
   - **Server output directories** (on autodl-5090 at `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/`):
     - `exp_018_qwen_scierc_seed456/` — report.json (verdict FAIL), run.log (correct path), samples.jsonl
     - `exp_018_qwen_scierc_seed123/` — report.json (verdict FAIL), NO run.log in dir (log at parent level)
     - `exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456/` — report.json (verdict FAIL), run.log (WRONG: says "saved to exp_007_llama_seed456"), samples.jsonl
     - `exp_018_llama_scierc_seed123/` — report.json (verdict FAIL), NO run.log in dir
   
   - **review_round2/qwen_scierc_3seed_summary.json**: Qwen 3-seed analysis output
     - NER: SJ ρ=0.364±0.018, FK=0.254±0.016, EM=0.307±0.008, logprob=0.217±0.013, VC=0.386±0.019
     - RE: SJ ρ=0.249±0.002, FK=0.290±0.012, logprob=0.297±0.022, VC=0.309±0.024
     - Data sources: seed42=exp_012_rerun_1024 (registered, max_tokens=1024), seeds 123/456=unregistered exp_018
   
   - **review_round2/compute_3seed_summary.py**: Analysis script for Qwen
     - SEED_DIRS mapping: {42: "exp_012_rerun_1024", 123: "exp_018_qwen_scierc_seed123", 456: "exp_018_qwen_scierc_seed456"}
     - Computes signals from raw samples.jsonl using consistency.py and evaluation.py
     - Sanity check: seed 42 ρ values match registry (SJ=0.3599, FK=0.2665, logprob=0.2052) but AUROC differs (script: 0.6116 vs registry: 0.5783 for logprob)
   
   - **review_round2/llama_scierc_3seed_summary.json**: LLaMA 3-seed analysis output
     - NER: SJ ρ=0.362±0.005, FK=0.309±0.015, EM=0.312±0.012, logprob=0.190±0.006, VC=0.402±0.012
     - RE: SJ ρ=0.277±0.022, FK=0.287±0.023, logprob=0.306±0.015, VC=0.330±0.012
   
   - **review_round2/compute_llama_3seed_summary.py**: Analysis script for LLaMA
     - SEED_DIRS: {42: "exp007_llama_inference", 123: "exp_018_llama_scierc_seed123", 456: "exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456"}
     - CRITICAL: seed 42 source `exp007_llama_inference` has NO --max_tokens (default 512, pre-D054)
   
   - **exp_007_llama_inference** (registry): command = `python run_mvp_pilot.py --model_path checkpoints/llama3.1-8b-scierc-merged ... --subtask joint --n_samples 8 --temperature 1.0 --seed 42 --num_test 9999 --collect_logprobs` — NO --max_tokens flag
   
   - **exp_012_rerun_1024** (registry): command includes `--max_tokens 1024` — correctly post-D054
   
   - **paper_framing.md**: Claim 1 positions SJ/EM/VC as "family of structural consistency signals." Evidence section from exp_012_rerun_1024. VC leads ρ (0.379), EM leads AUROC (0.749), SJ positioned for generalizability. Target: EMNLP 2026 Main, ARR May 2026-05-25.
   
   - **report.md**: Shows all 4 GPUs idle, no running experiments, 73 registry entries (37 success, 18 negative, 1 failed)

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Registry filter by nested field failed**: `filters={"spec.dataset": "SciERC"}` returned 0 results — nested field filtering not supported. Worked around by fetching all entries.
   - **ssh_read_only pipe character blocked**: Commands with `|` rejected by whitelist. Used `grep` directly without pipes.
   - **exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456 log provenance mismatch**: run.log in this directory actually contains output from exp_007_llama_seed456 (different experiment). Timestamp 05-14 23:08 vs directory update 05-15 13:08. Flagged in review.
   - **AUROC computation discrepancy**: The 3-seed analysis script's `compute_auroc` produces different AUROC values from the pipeline's registered metrics (e.g., logprob AUROC 0.6116 vs 0.5783 for same data). Spearman ρ values match perfectly. Noted but not yet fully resolved.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Registry gap identified and escalated**: Found 4 exp_018 experiments with no registry entries. Posted review to Director. A provenance worker was dispatched to fix this but the registry still shows 73 entries (no exp_018 added) — fix appears to have failed.
   - **max_tokens inconsistency discovered**: LLaMA 3-seed analysis mixes pre-D054 (max_tokens=512) and post-D054 data. Qwen analysis correctly uses post-D054 data for seed 42. Recommended LLaMA seed 42 rerun with max_tokens=1024.
   - **VC > SJ pattern identified**: Voting Confidence consistently outperforms Soft Jaccard in NER ρ for both models (Qwen: 0.386>0.364, LLaMA: 0.402>0.362). Flagged as paper framing concern — reviewers may question SJ's positioning.
   - **Cross-model consistency confirmed**: SJ ρ remarkably stable across models (0.364 vs 0.362), supporting model-agnostic claim.

6. All User Messages:
   - `[系统通知 — worker_done:worker_inference_qwen_scierc_seed456_20260515T050627]`: Worker completed, review requested.
   - "Continue from where you left off." — after Qwen 3-seed analysis review was partially done.
   - `[系统通知 — worker_done:worker_analysis_3seed_qwen_scierc_20260515T051603]`: Second worker completed.
   - "Continue from where you left off." — after I read paper_framing.md for context.
   - `[服务器重启后首条消息]` + `[系统通知 — worker_done:worker_analysis_3seed_llama_scierc_20260515T060020]`: Server restart, refresh state, review LLaMA analysis.
   - "Continue from where you left off." — no action needed after LLaMA review posted.
   - `[系统通知 — worker_done:worker_provenance_exp_018_registry_20260515T055957]`: Provenance worker completed.

7. Pending Tasks:
   - **Verify whether the provenance worker actually fixed the registry gap**: My last check showed registry still at 73 entries with no exp_018. Need to post a follow-up review noting the fix failed.
   - **Ongoing monitoring**: exp_018 registry gap remains unresolved; LLaMA seed 42 max_tokens inconsistency remains unresolved.

8. Current Work:
   I had just checked the registry after the `worker_provenance_exp_018_registry` worker completed (status=done). The registry still shows exactly 73 entries with zero exp_018 entries added. The provenance worker appears to have completed without actually creating the expected registry entries.
